Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for imperfect-information card games are only as strong as the opponents they train against, and they are hard to grade, since they beat a random opponent over 99 percent of the time and only tie copies of themselves. So we build a strong, fixed, rule-based expert for Gin Rummy and use it only as a yardstick, never for training. It beats every agent we trained 70 to 99 percent of the time. Across more than a hundred runs, we isolate what makes a lightweight agent stronger. Trust region updates, a well-aimed reward, a curriculum of tougher opponents, warm starting, and keeping the best checkpoint all help, and stacking them lifts a self-play champion from about 30 to 36 percent against the expert. Several ideas did not pay off. Short-term and longer-term reward shaping, learned state embeddings, imitation and DAgger, and a live large language model opponent were each unhelpful, too slow, or too heavy to train at scale. Comparing MLP, convolutional, set-based, attention, and recurrent encoders shows that extra capacity does little to break the ceiling, suggesting the limit is information rather than network size. We add standard baselines (neural fictitious self-play and information set Monte Carlo search) and confirm the approach carries over to Leduc Hold'em, where the optimum is computable. The result is a lightweight, game-agnostic recipe that trains competitive agents without training on the expert, for any game a small model can handle, reported with robust statistics and released as a reusable package.

</details>


### [38] [Geometric Self-Distillation for Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06855)
*Josip Jukić,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoSD方法，通过几何自蒸馏目标（Hellinger损失+Fisher-Rao近端约束）解决特权上下文自蒸馏中教师过度自信导致的学生分布外（OOD）推理退化问题，在数学推理任务上平均提升OOD准确率5.7-8.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏虽然能通过教师密集监督提升学生模型性能，但在特权上下文设置下（教师看到完整解题线索而学生看不到），教师的信任度不可靠——它对某些token的偏好依赖于自己拥有的特权信息，学生无法仅凭前缀合理生成这些token。这导致蒸馏集中在教师与学生分歧最大的地方，累积成分布漂移（drift），最终损害分布外（OOD）推理能力。

Method: GeoSD包含两个互补的几何约束：(1) Hellinger损失，按学生与教师当前分布的重叠程度缩放教师偏好，减弱学生对无法支持的token的学习压力；(2) Fisher-Rao近端项，惩罚学生预测分布与最近检查点之间的漂移距离。两个距离都定义在下一个token分布构成的几何空间中，使用自然梯度更新在该几何空间而非参数空间中更新。

Result: 在数学推理基准和三个模型家族（1.7B-32B）上，GeoSD保持了自蒸馏的分布内增益，同时平均OOD准确率比基座模型提升5.7-8.6个百分点。分析表明标准匹配方法通过从高熵状态中抽取概率质量来达成与教师的一致，导致对错误答案的自信一致，而GeoSD则保持这些替代选项的可达性。

Conclusion: GeoSD通过几何自蒸馏有效解决了特权上下文自蒸馏中的分布漂移问题，为OOD推理提供了显著改进，同时保留了分布内性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is a practical post-training recipe for large language models, supplying dense teacher supervision on the student's own trajectories. In privileged-context self-distillation, teacher and student are the same model conditioned on the same prefix, but the teacher also sees a hint or the full solution trace. This makes supervision abundant but harder to trust: the teacher can be confident about continuations its privileged view makes obvious but the student cannot yet justify. The distillation pull is strongest where teacher and student disagree most, and over many updates it accumulates into drift that degrades out-of-distribution (OOD) reasoning. We introduce GeoSD, a geometric self-distillation objective that treats this drift as movement in the student's predictive behavior and counters it in two complementary ways. A Hellinger loss scales each teacher preference by the overlap the student already shares with it, attenuating the pull on tokens the student cannot yet support. Since these pulls still compound over training, a proximal term penalizes how far the student's predictions drift from a recent checkpoint, measured as a Fisher-Rao distance. Both are distances in the same geometry of next-token distributions, and a natural-gradient update takes its steps in that geometry rather than in parameter space. Across mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model families, GeoSD preserves the in-distribution gains of self-distillation while improving average OOD accuracy by 5.7-8.6 points over the base model, with gains holding across model scales from 1.7B to 32B. Analyzing why standard matching fails out of distribution, we find it wins agreement with the teacher by draining mass from alternatives at high-entropy states, resulting in confident agreement on wrong answers, whereas GeoSD keeps those alternatives in reach.

</details>


### [39] [Best-Arm Identification with Generative Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06879)
*Tianyi Ma,Hanzhang Qin,Ruihao Zhu,Jierui Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE算法，在固定置信度的最优臂识别问题中，利用廉价代理分数（如ML/LLM预测）与昂贵奖励的相关性来降低样本复杂度，通过普通最小二乘法在线学习未知相关系数并保持统计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优臂识别中每次奖励观测成本高昂，而机器学习和大语言模型可以提供廉价但相关的代理分数。如何利用这些代理分数来降低样本复杂度，同时处理未知相关系数的在线学习问题，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出PROBE算法：将问题转化为异方差识别问题，采用控制变量调整；使用阶段消除（phase-elimination）框架，通过普通最小二乘（OLS）拟合直接维护残差方差的上确界（upper certificate），利用精确卡方分布确保置信界不依赖于未知相关系数。

Result: PROBE是δ-PAC的，其样本复杂度在常数乘子与常数校准代价内达到已知相关系数的oracle复杂度；算法可最小改动地扩展到(ε,δ)-PAC设置；在合成数据和真实汽车贷款定价重放实验（使用LLM和表格代理）中，样本节省量与奖励-代理相关性强度成正比，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: PROBE算法有效利用廉价代理分数降低了最优臂识别的样本成本，且无需预先知道代理与奖励的相关系数，理论保证和实验验证均表明其性能随相关性增强而提升。

Abstract: Best-arm identification is a canonical model for data-driven decision-making, but in many applications each reward observation is costly. Motivated by the growing availability of cheap predictions from machine learning and large language models, we study fixed-confidence best-arm identification in which each costly reward pull is paired with a cheap but correlated proxy score. The marginal mean of the proxy can be estimated offline and is treated as known, whereas its correlation $ρ$ with the reward, which governs how much the proxy helps, is unknown and must be learned online in pair with real rewards. We show that a control-variate adjustment turns this model into a heteroscedastic identification problem whose oracle sample complexity improves by residual variance $1-ρ^2$. The central difficulty is that the correlation must be learned from the same costly samples that identification consumes online, and that a plug-in estimate of the residual variance is anti-conservative and can compromise correctness. We propose PROBE (PRoxy OLS for Best-arm Exploration), a phase-elimination algorithm that directly maintains an upper certificate on the residual variance with an ordinary least squares fit, whose exact chi-square law keeps the certificate valid regardless of the unknown correlation. We prove that PROBE is $δ$-PAC and attains the known-correlation oracle sample complexity up to a constant multiplicative factor and a constant additive calibration cost. The guarantee extends to the $(ε,δ)$-PAC setting under minimal changes to the algorithm. Numerical experiments on synthetic instances and on an auto-loan pricing replay with large language model and tabular proxies confirm that the sample savings of PROBE scale with the strength of the reward-proxy correlation, exactly as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [40] [Converge to Surprise: Evolutionary Self-supervised Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06887)
*Canlin Zhang,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度下降、无需预定义目标的自监督图像聚类框架，通过最大化“惊喜分数”迫使模型拒绝最大熵零假设，并采用进化策略与梯度下降内外环相结合的方式优化，在非参数自监督图像聚类任务上取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督图像聚类方法几乎全部依赖梯度下降，每一步优化都需要明确的目标（如对比对、掩码块、伪标签等），这限制了模型在无先验知识情况下发现非随机特征的能力。作者希望摆脱这种对预定义目标的依赖，提出一种更通用的自监督框架。

Method: 1) 以最大熵原理下的像素独立同分布作为零假设H0；2) 定义衡量模型输出在H0下出现概率的“惊喜分数”；3) 通过最大化惊喜分数迫使模型拒绝H0，从而发现数据的非随机特征；4) 采用“收敛到惊喜”优化策略：外层进化策略直接最大化惊喜分数，内层周期性梯度下降以ES发现的聚类作为代理目标进行优化。

Result: 在标准图像基准上，该框架在非参数自监督图像聚类（最严格的深度聚类设置，模型不知道真实类别数）中取得了新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一种不依赖梯度下降和预定义目标的自监督学习范式是可行的，为深度聚类提供了一种全新的优化框架。

Abstract: Most self-supervised image clustering models, actually almost all deep learning approaches, are based on gradient descent: In order to calculate the loss, every optimization step requires a clearly defined target, whether a contrastive split, a masked patch or entity, an EMA-teacher output, a pseudo-label, or a differentiable information-theoretic functional. We propose a self-supervised framework that drops this requirement for image clustering. Without any prior knowledge, we have to assume that each pixel is i.i.d. according to the Principle of Maximum Entropy. Taking this as our null hypothesis H0, we define a "surprise score" that measures how unlikely the model's output representation would be under H0. Maximizing the surprise score forces the deep learning model to reject H0 - equivalently, to discover non-random feature from data. Also, here is our fundamental assumption: a surprise score cannot, in general, be reduced to a per-step loss. Hence, we propose the "converge-to-surprise" scheme to optimize our model: an evolution-strategy (ES) outer loop, which directly maximizes the surprise score without needing its gradient, paired with a periodic gradient-descent inner loop, which uses the surprising clusters already discovered by ES as surrogate targets. On standard image benchmarks, our framework achieves new state-of-the-art results in non-parametric self-supervised image clustering - the strictest deep-clustering setting, in which the number of ground-truth classes is not given to the model.

</details>


### [41] [Latency-Constrained DNN Architecture Learning for Edge Systems using Zerorized Batch Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06922)
*Shuo Huai,Di Liu,Hao Kong,Weichen Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向延迟的神经网络学习方法，通过通用硬件定制延迟预测器，在满足严格延迟约束的同时优化模型精度，仅需一次训练即可完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的深度学习应用面临延迟约束，而传统神经网络优化方法无法直接改变模型推理的时间成本，缺乏面向延迟约束的模型优化手段。

Method: 提出延迟导向的神经网络学习方法，引入通用硬件定制延迟预测器，通过一次性训练过程优化模型，使其在满足延迟约束的同时保持高精度。

Result: 在ImageNet-100数据集和34ms延迟约束下，GoogLeNet延迟从40.32ms降至34ms，精度仅降低0.14%；结合量化后精度仅降0.04%。VGG-19从119.98ms压缩至34ms，精度提升0.5%；GoogLeNet从20.27ms扩展至34ms，精度提升0.78%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效满足严格的延迟约束并实现高精度，优于现有方法，且框架已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning applications have been widely adopted on edge devices, to mitigate the privacy and latency issues of accessing cloud servers. Deciding the number of neurons during the design of a deep neural network to maximize performance is not intuitive. Particularly, many application scenarios are real-time and have a strict latency constraint, while conventional neural network optimization methods do not directly change the temporal cost of model inference for latency-critical edge systems. In this work, we propose a latency-oriented neural network learning method to optimize models for high accuracy while fulfilling the latency constraint. For efficiency, we also introduce a universal hardware-customized latency predictor to optimize this procedure to learn a model that satisfies the latency constraint by only a one-shot training process. The experiment results reveal that, compared to state-of-the-art methods, our approach can well-fit the 'hard' latency constraint and achieve high accuracy. Under the same training settings as the original model and satisfying a 34 ms latency constraint on the ImageNet-100 dataset, we reduce GoogLeNet's latency from 40.32 ms to 34 ms with a 0.14% accuracy reduction on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano. When coupled with quantization, our method can be further improved to only 0.04% drop for GoogLeNet. On the NVIDIA Jetson TX2, we compress VGG-19 from 119.98 ms to 34 ms and even improve its accuracy by 0.5%, and we scale GoogLeNet up from 20.27 ms to 34 ms and achieve higher accuracy by 0.78%. We also open source this framework at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/ZeroBN

</details>


### [42] [Intrinsic-Noise Consolidation: A Doob-Barrier-Conditioned Diffusion Turns Analog Device Noise into a Continual-Learning Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06924)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在模拟神经形态硬件上，利用器件固有噪声通过Doob h-变换实现突触巩固，将噪声从精度负担转化为记忆巩固红利。实验表明，屏障条件化在Split-MNIST上提升保留率10.9点，且在BrainScaleS-2芯片上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为模拟神经形态硬件中的固有噪声会损害精度，本文试图反转这一认知，探索能否利用噪声来巩固记忆，从而将噪声成本转化为收益。

Method: 将每个突触的随机动力学建模为Doob h-变换，条件化权重永远不会跨越围绕其巩固值的记忆关键屏障。条件化扩散获得额外漂移项sigma^2 d/dw log h，该恢复力与噪声方差成正比并在屏障处发散。规则包含锚定漂移-s(w-mu)部分（来自OUA、MESU、EWC），核心创新在于屏障条件化的突触规则及其预测：增加固有噪声会非单调地提升序列任务保留率（倒U型曲线）。

Result: 在单头Split-MNIST上（8个种子），规则在内部最优点提升保留率10.9个点（配对Wilcoxon p=0.004），而OU/EWC/MESU锚定方法呈单调变化。消融实验去除条件化后效果消失；最优值随屏障移动；倒U型曲线在第二任务流和前向传播含噪声的场景中仍然存在。在BrainScaleS-2芯片上，屏障条件化保留先前任务比匹配控制好15.6个点。

Conclusion: 模拟固有噪声可转化为巩固红利，而数字加速器需要消耗能量才能产生这种效果。屏障条件化实现了稳定性-可塑性权衡的偏移，而非净准确率的提升。

Abstract: On analog neuromorphic hardware, intrinsic device noise is normally an accuracy tax. We ask whether it can instead consolidate memories. We cast per-synapse consolidation as a Doob h-transform: condition each weight's stochastic dynamics on never crossing a memory-critical barrier around its consolidated value. The conditioned diffusion gains an extra drift sigma^2 d/dw log h, a restoring force amplified by the noise variance itself that diverges at the barrier. We are explicit about novelty: the anchored drift -s(w-mu) our rule also contains is not ours (the limit of OUA, MESU, and EWC), and we surrender it. We claim only the conjunction of (a) the Doob barrier-conditioning as a synaptic rule, to our knowledge unclaimed (every h-transform use we found is generative modeling, none synaptic), and (b) a falsifiable prediction: increasing intrinsic noise non-monotonically improves sequential-task retention, an inverted-U that anchored-drift methods cannot produce. We pre-registered this as a go/no-go gate; it passes. On single-head Split-MNIST (8 seeds) the rule lifts retention 10.9 points at an interior optimum (paired Wilcoxon p=0.004), while matched OU/EWC/MESU anchors are monotone. Ablating the conditioning removes the effect; the optimum tracks the barrier; the inverted-U survives a second task stream and the realization where noise enters the forward pass. We then measure the intrinsic noise on real BrainScaleS-2 silicon (additive, trial-to-trial independent, tunable via on-chip averaging) and run the rule on the chip with its noise in the training loop: barrier-conditioning retains a prior task 15.6 points better than the matched control at matched average accuracy, a stability-plasticity shift, not a net-accuracy win (single seed; retention measured, energy modelled). Intrinsic analog noise thus becomes a consolidation dividend a digital accelerator must spend energy to generate.

</details>


### [43] [Imputation Meets Clustering: Exploiting Latent Subgroup Structure for Missing Data Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06930)
*Chuyao Zhang,E Li,Taochen Chen,Yiqun Zhang,Yuzhu Ji,Shuping Zhao,Peng Liu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAGI提出了一种聚类感知的生成式插补框架，通过"分区-引导-恢复"策略将聚类与插补联合优化，利用动态聚类分配作为局部先验指导生成对抗网络，在14个基准数据集上优于15个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插补方法忽略了实际数据中存在的群体异质性（多个子群体具有不同分布），在缺乏显式结构指导时会产生模糊子群体边界的通用估计，且融入子群体信息面临循环依赖：可靠的子群体识别需要完整数据，而数据补全本身就是插补目标。

Method: CAGI采用"Partition-Guide-Restore"策略：1) Partition阶段动态聚类分配生成局部先验；2) Guide阶段将聚类信息作为条件输入生成对抗网络（GAN）；3) 建立迭代反馈循环逐步优化聚类结构和插补值；4) 结合实例级重建与分布级正则化的多层级优化目标。

Result: 在14个基准数据集上，与15个代表性基线方法相比，CAGI在插补性能上表现出显著优越性。

Conclusion: CAGI通过将聚类与插补构建为相互增强的协同优化过程，有效解决了群体异质性数据的插补问题，为复杂结构数据的预处理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Missing data is prevalent in practical applications, making effective imputation an essential preprocessing step for downstream analysis. Real-world datasets often exhibit complex latent structures composed of multiple subgroups with distinct distributions. However, existing methods often overlook such population heterogeneity. Without explicit structural guidance, these methods tend to produce generic estimates that blur subgroup boundaries and lack instance-level fidelity. While incorporating subgroup information offers a remedy, it faces a circular dependency: reliable subgroup identification requires complete data, while data completion is the imputation objective itself. To resolve this, we propose CAGI (Cluster-Aware Generative Imputation), a framework that reformulates clustering and imputation as a mutually reinforcing co-optimization process. CAGI employs a ``Partition-Guide-Restore'' strategy where dynamic cluster assignments act as local priors to condition a Generative Adversarial Network. An iterative feedback loop is established to progressively refine both cluster structures and imputed values toward faithful subgroup distributions. To ensure distributional stability, CAGI further employs a multi-level optimization objective combining instance-level reconstruction with distribution-level regularization. Extensive experiments on 14 benchmark datasets with 15 representative baselines demonstrate the superiority of CAGI. The source code is available at: https://github.com/supercocachii/CAGI

</details>


### [44] [Physical activities enable scalable foundation modelling for broad-spectrum health prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06954)
*Zhenghuang Wu,Yuyao Zhu,Songlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StepFM，一种仅基于步数计数数据的Foundation Model，用于多种健康风险预测。StepFM利用步数数据的普适性和低维特性，提供了一种隐私保护、计算高效的替代方案。通过大规模步数序列的预训练框架，StepFM可迁移至20多种健康风险预测任务，在异构设备、新区域和新疾病类型上均表现优异，并揭示了身体活动模式与健康风险之间的可解释关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感模型多为特定疾病类型设计，跨健康风险的迁移能力有限；而现有的Wearable Foundation Model依赖高频原始传感器数据，存在隐私、计算开销和跨设备/人群可扩展性的问题。步数数据虽然维度低、普适性强，但尚未被充分用于构建通用健康推理模型。

Method: StepFM构建在低维步数计数数据之上，设计了一个可扩展的预训练框架，从大规模步数序列中捕获时间动态和行为模式，并通过迁移学习支持超过20种健康风险预测任务。

Result: 实验表明StepFM在多种健康风险预测任务上达到与现有方法相当的强性能，且在异构设备、新区域和新型疾病类型上保持鲁棒性；分析进一步揭示了身体活动模式与多种健康风险之间的可解释关系。

Conclusion: 步数传感可作为可扩展、真实世界健康监测的可行基础，StepFM为实现通用化、隐私保护的Wearable Foundation Model提供了新路径。

Abstract: Wearable and mobile sensing technologies have demonstrated strong potential for health inference; however, most sensor models are designed for specific disease types, limiting their transferability across different health risks. Wearable foundation models offer a more generalizable approach in diverse health risk types. Nevertheless, most existing methods rely on high-frequency raw sensor data, raising concerns about privacy, computational overhead, and scalability across devices and populations. In this paper, we propose StepFM, a foundation model built solely on step counter data for broad-spectrum health prediction. Leveraging the ubiquity and low-dimensional nature of step data, StepFM provides a practical, privacy-preserving, and computation-efficient alternative to traditional sensor-based models. We design a scalable pre-training framework that captures temporal dynamics and behavioral patterns from large-scale step sequences, enabling transfer across more than 20 health risk prediction tasks spanning diverse devices, new regions, and novel disease types. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StepFM achieves strong performance compared to existing methods while maintaining robustness across heterogeneous settings. Furthermore, our analysis reveals interpretable and generalizable relationships between physical activity patterns and various health risks, offering new insights into activity-based health modeling. Our work establishes step-based sensing as a viable foundation for scalable and real-world health monitoring.

</details>


### [45] [Rethinking Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06973)
*Haoxin Liu,Yichen Zhou,Rajat Sen,B. Aditya Prakash,Abhimanyu Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TimesX，一个包含多样化真实世界时间序列和丰富文本上下文的新基准，用于多模态时间序列预测，解决了现有基准泛化差、上下文类型有限和数据泄漏等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列预测基准存在三个主要问题：(1) 数据规模小且多为合成数据，导致泛化能力差；(2) 基准中文本上下文的类型非常有限；(3) 无法缓解评估中的数据泄漏问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含跨领域高质量真实世界时间序列及自动化生成的丰富文本上下文的数据集TimesX，并在该基准上对多种零样本多模态预测方法进行了全面的实证研究。

Result: 在许多现有基准上表现良好的方法在TimesX上可能失效；而利用丰富文本上下文的简单集成方法可以超越强基线方法。

Conclusion: TimesX作为更具挑战性和现实意义的多模态时间序列预测基准，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并证明了融合文本上下文信息的简单集成策略的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce a new context-enriched, multimodal time series forecasting benchmark, TimesX. TimesX contains a wide selection of high-quality real-world time series with diverse domains and textual contexts obtained from an automated data generation pipeline, which helps address three main issues of existing multimodal forecasting benchmarks: (1) poor generalization due to the small scale and synthetic nature of benchmark data, (2) very limited types of textual contexts in the benchmarks, and (3) an inability to mitigate data leakage in evaluation. We conduct a thorough empirical study of zero-shot multimodal forecasting approaches on TimesX. Our results suggest that many approaches that perform well on existing benchmarks may fail on TimesX. In contrast, simple ensemble methods that leverage rich textual context accompanying time-series can outperform strong baselines on TimesX.

</details>


### [46] [Hybrid Least Squares/Gradient Descent Methods for MIONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06976)
*Jun Choi,Chang-Ock Lee,Minam Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于MIONet的高效混合最小二乘/梯度下降（LSGD）方法，通过交替最小二乘法优化各分支网络的最后一层参数，并利用Kronecker和Khatri-Rao积等技巧处理大规模矩阵，加速训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIONet训练速度慢，需要加速方法。现有LSGD方法仅适用于DeepONet，需要推广到更一般的MIONet架构。

Method: 将MIONet视为关于各分支网络最后一层参数的多线性函数，采用交替最小二乘法依次优化每个分支网络的参数；利用Kronecker积、Khatri-Rao积和张量置换矩阵将大规模系统矩阵分解为小矩阵，提高计算效率；该方法兼容带有正则化项的通用L^2损失函数。

Result: LSGD方法成功推广到MIONet，通过交替最小二乘和矩阵分解技术实现了高效训练。

Conclusion: 提出的LSGD方法能够有效加速MIONet训练，且兼容带线性算子的通用L^2损失及正则化项。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an efficient hybrid least squares/gradient descent (LSGD) method for MIONets to accelerate training. This method generalizes the LSGD method for DeepONets. Since MIONet is the sum of the entrywise product of multiple branch networks and a trunk network, it can be viewed as a multilinear function with respect to the last layer parameters of each branch network. These sets of parameters can be optimized using the alternating least squares method, where we solve the LS system for a single branch network in turn. To handle the large-sized system matrix, we introduce Kronecker and Khatri-Rao products and tensor permutation matrices to factor the large matrix into small ones. Our method is compatible with a general type of $L^2$ loss with regularization terms for the last layer parameters of each branch, where linear operators can be applied to the MIONet output in each loss term.

</details>


### [47] [Robust Federated Learning Under Real-World Client Churn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06979)
*Dhruv Garg,Neha Lakhani,Debopam Sanyal,Myungjin Lee,Alexey Tumanov,Ada Gavrilovska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FeLiX是一个联邦学习编排框架，通过流式感知可用性分层、新鲜效用选择和延迟鲁棒聚合三种原语，在动态数据流场景下将达到目标精度的挂钟时间缩短2.37倍，同时降低1.30倍通信带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统在生产部署中面临多天更新周期导致模型陈旧的问题，无法快速适应由病毒式趋势和用户意图变化驱动的易变数据分布，而现有FL系统忽略了三个挑战：瞬态客户端可用性、动态数据异构性以及模型预测与可观察结果之间的延迟。

Method: FeLiX提出三种原语：(i) 流式感知可用性分层，利用轻量级遥测大规模识别就绪客户端；(ii) 新鲜效用选择，双层级机制优先选择能在紧迫更新期限内完成、具有统计价值的设备更新；(iii) 信息感知延迟鲁棒聚合，融入包含真实结果的高价值延迟更新，同时避免全局模型偏向陈旧分布。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Google Speech和真实低可用性轨迹数据集上，FeLiX相比最先进的同步和异步FL基线，将到达目标精度的挂钟时间缩短最多2.37倍，同时减少1.30倍通信带宽。

Conclusion: FeLiX在不依赖不现实的预言式客户端知识的情况下，在真实场景中实现了接近预言式的性能，有效弥合了联邦学习模型新鲜度与实际部署需求之间的差距。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables training shared models on private, on-device data, but production deployments remain constrained to slow, multi-day refresh cycles due to the complexity of coordinating massive client populations. For applications such as feed ranking, ad targeting, and personalized recommendation, model freshness: the ability to rapidly adapt to new user-local data is critical for maximizing objectives like click-through rate. This lag leaves models stale and unresponsive to volatile data distributions driven by viral trends and shifting user intent. Bridging this gap requires addressing three challenges overlooked by existing FL systems: transient client availability, dynamic data heterogeneity, and delays between model predictions and observable outcomes. We present FeLiX, an FL orchestration framework that minimizes wall-clock time-to-target accuracy on live interaction streams. FeLiX introduces three primitives: (i) streaming-aware availability tiers that leverage lightweight telemetry to identify ready clients at scale; (ii) fresh-utility selection, a dual-tier mechanism that prioritizes statistically valuable updates from devices able to meet tight refresh deadlines; and (iii) informativeness-aware, delay-robust aggregation that incorporates late, high-value updates containing ground-truth outcomes without biasing the global model toward stale distributions. Unlike prior systems that rely on unrealistic oracular knowledge of client availability, FeLiX achieves near-oracular performance in real-world settings. Across CIFAR-10, Google Speech, and realistic low-availability traces, FeLiX reduces wall-clock time-to-target accuracy by up to 2.37X while reducing communication bandwidth by 1.30X compared to state-of-the-art synchronous and asynchronous FL baselines.

</details>


### [48] [UP: Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization for Breaking the Exploration-Stability Dilemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06987)
*Chongyu Fan,Pengfei Liu,Jingjia Huang,Sijia Liu,Yi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP)，一种即插即用的优化目标，通过停止梯度操作锚定当前策略，对正优势提供无裁剪稳定梯度以最大化探索，对负优势保持标准裁剪防止不稳定，从而解决强化学习中的探索-稳定性困境，在多种RL算法、模型架构和训练模态上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重要性采样的RL框架存在探索-稳定性困境：纯IS导致训练不稳定，而标准裁剪机制虽能缓解不稳定但会过早截断低置信度但正确推理路径的更新预算，结构性抑制探索。

Method: 提出Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP)：通过stop-gradient算子将策略锚定在当前状态，对正优势使用无裁剪稳定梯度以最大化探索能力，对负优势保持标准裁剪以防止训练不稳定。方法可即插即用于token级（GRPO、DAPO）和序列级（GSPO）优化粒度。

Result: 在DAPO、GSPO、GRPO等RL算法，Dense、MoE等模型架构，以及语言和多模态训练模态上，UP均提升了探索能力和推理准确率。

Conclusion: UP是一种通用的即插即用增强方法，能有效克服裁剪机制对探索的限制，提升RL训练推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the standard paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). To achieve sample efficiency, modern RL frameworks rely on importance sampling (IS). However, these algorithms suffer from an exploration-stability dilemma. Pure IS often leads to catastrophic training instability, while standard clipping mechanisms used to mitigate this instability strictly constrain the policy update budget. By formalizing the concept of Probability Capacity (Cap), we reveal that conservative clipping structurally stifles exploration by prematurely truncating the update budget for correct but low-confidence reasoning paths. To break free from these constraints, we propose Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP), a universal and plug-and-play objective. UP theoretically restructures the optimization process by anchoring the policy to its current state via the stop-gradient operator. This asymmetric design unleashes unclipped, stable gradients for positive advantages to maximize exploration, while maintaining standard clipping safeguards for negative advantages to prevent training instability. Furthermore, our formulation readily extends across different optimization granularities, including token-level (GRPO, DAPO) and sequence-level (GSPO) frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UP enhances exploration capacity and achieves superior reasoning accuracy across diverse RL algorithms (DAPO, GSPO, and GRPO), model architectures (Dense, MoE, and vision-language), and training modalities (language and multimodal), validating UP as a truly universal plug-and-play enhancement for RL-based training.

</details>


### [49] [Physics-guided spatiotemporal neural models for fuel density prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06999)
*Tolga Caglar,Jaynil Jaiswal,Saqib Azim,Yudhir Gala,Mai H. Nguyen,Ilkay Altintas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的机器学习（PGML）框架用于燃料密度预测，通过将物理约束和领域知识融入深度学习模型（ConvLSTM、AFNONet、ViViT），在损失函数中加入可微的物理信息项（质量守恒燃料传输项和蔓延速率估计），实验表明该框架在精度和稳定性上均优于纯数据驱动的基线模型，为自适应计划烧除管理提供了高效且物理合理的火灾预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的深度学习模型在燃料密度预测中缺乏物理约束，导致预测结果可能违反物理规律（如质量不守恒），且精度和稳定性不足。因此需要将物理知识融入模型以提升预测的物理合理性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了PGML框架，采用三种深度学习架构（ConvLSTM、AFNONet、ViViT）建模燃料密度的时空演化；在损失函数中引入可微分的物理信息项，包括质量守恒的燃料传输项和蔓延速率估计项，实现物理约束的端到端学习。

Result: 在多次独立试验中，所提出的PGML框架在预测精度和稳定性上均优于未施加物理约束的纯数据驱动基线模型。

Conclusion: 该PGML框架能够实现计算高效且物理合理的火灾预测，适用于辅助自适应计划烧除管理决策。

Abstract: This paper presents a physics-guided machine learning (PGML) framework for fuel density prediction, integrating physics constraints and domain knowledge into deep learning models to enhance model accuracy and stability. We explore three deep learning architectures -- ConvLSTM, Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator (AFNONet), and Video Vision Transformer (ViViT) -- to model the spatiotemporal evolution of fuel density. Our approach incorporates differentiable physics-informed terms in the loss function, including a mass-conserving fuel transport term and a rate-of-spread estimation. Experimental results, averaged across multiple independent trials, demonstrate that the proposed PGML framework outperforms purely data-driven baselines without physics constraints in both accuracy and stability. This framework enables computationally efficient, physically plausible fire forecasting to support adaptive prescribed burn management.

</details>


### [50] [Dissociating the Internal Representations of Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07003)
*Anthony Baez,Sheer Karny,Pat Pataranutaporn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中的谄媚行为（sycophancy）可分解为事实型（factual）和观点型（opinion）两种子类型，并通过探针和特征向量干预实验揭示了不同模型对这两种子类型的内部分表徵存在统一或分离的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM中的谄媚行为已被广泛研究，但其内部机制是否反映该行为的多面性（即不同子类型）尚不清楚。本文旨在拆解谄媚行为的表徵结构，探究事实型与观点型谄媚是否共享内部分表徵。

Method: 将谄媚行为分为事实型（基于可验证事实）和观点型（基于主观信念）两种子类型；分别在一种子类型的激活上训练线性探针和构建干预特征向量，然后评估其对另一种子类型的迁移效果，以此衡量两种子类型是否共享内部分表徵。

Result: 不同LLM对这两种子类型的表徵方式不同：有些模型的表徵更统一（探针和干预可跨子类型迁移），有些模型的表徵更分离且存在因果干扰。

Conclusion: 该解耦方法为研究复杂模型行为的内部分表徵结构提供了一个有前景的分析框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit sycophancy, where they agree with a user's statement even when incorrect. While sycophancy is often treated as a single defined behavior, it can manifest in substantially distinct ways and circumstances, raising the question of whether this multi-faceted nature is reflected in its internal mechanisms. To address this gap, we dissociate the representations of sycophancy into factual and opinion subtypes -- motivated by the distinction between verifiable claims and subjective beliefs. We train linear probes and construct steering vectors on activations of one subtype and evaluate their transfer to the other subtype to measure to what extent they share representations. We find evidence that different LLMs represent these subtypes differently, with either more unified or more distinct and causally interfering representations. This method of dissociation offers a promising framework for studying the representational structure of complex model behaviors.

</details>


### [51] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal-Frequency Fusion with Peak Enhancement for Cellular Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07016)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSPF-Net，一种融合外部新闻上下文信息的多模态蜂窝网络流量预测框架，通过联合建模时空-频谱流量模式、突发峰值特征和城市新闻事件信号，在多个真实数据集上显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空流量预测方法大多关注单一模态的内在模式或结构关系，未能有效建模突发性内生动态以及外部城市事件引发的扰动，导致预测精度不足。

Method: 提出MSPF-Net框架，包含四个核心模块：(1) 时空-频谱流量编码器，捕捉时间、空间和频谱维度的流量模式；(2) 峰值增强模块，提取突发尖峰的感知表征；(3) 新闻上下文表示模块，将城市新闻流编码为外源上下文嵌入；(4) 动态融合预测模块，自适应整合异质信号进行预测。

Result: 在Milano、Trento和LTE三个真实流量数据集上的实验表明，联合建模流量动态、突发模式和新闻上下文信号能有效提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 融合外部多模态上下文信息（如新闻事件）对于改进蜂窝网络流量预测具有重要价值，未来可进一步探索更多类型的异质外源信号。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of cellular network traffic is essential for network planning, resource allocation, and quality-of-service assurance in modern mobile communication systems. Real-world traffic often exhibits bursty endogenous dynamics and disturbances triggered by external urban events, which makes reliable prediction highly challenging. Most existing spatiotemporal traffic forecasting methods primarily focus on intrinsic traffic patterns or structural relationships within a single modality, and rarely model burst behavior together with exogenous contextual signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{MSPF-Net}, a multimodal cellular traffic forecasting framework that integrates external contextual information. Specifically, MSPF-Net consists of a Spatiotemporal-Frequency Traffic Encoder for capturing temporal, spatial, and spectral traffic patterns, a Peak Enhancement Module for extracting burst-aware representations of sudden spikes, a News Context Representation Module for encoding urban news streams into exogenous contextual embeddings, and a Dynamic Fusion Prediction Module for adaptively integrating these heterogeneous signals to generate forecasts. Experiments on the Milano, Trento, and LTE traffic datasets demonstrate that jointly modeling traffic dynamics, burst patterns, and news contextual signals can effectively improve forecasting performance.

</details>


### [52] [Online Data Selection Is Implicit Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07023)
*Aoxiong Zeng,Yuxin Yang,Xiangquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线数据选择作为隐式对齐机制的现象，发现SFT阶段的数据选择已经会改变模型的行为偏好（如拒绝率、冗长度等），无需偏好优化。提出Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA)协议用于量化选择导致的行为偏移，以及Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS)诊断性选择器用于在保持数据效率的同时约束安全与风格方向上的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"SFT仅做能力适应、对齐属于偏好优化阶段"的传统认知，证明在线数据选择本身就构成隐式对齐机制，但缺乏对选择导致的行为偏移的系统性量化和理解。

Method: 将在线数据选择形式化为重加权SFT目标，其中权重定义了对响应风格和安全姿态的隐式偏好。比较随机、基于损失、基于质量和基于多样性的在线选择器，在保持相同基础模型、优化器和选定token预算下，测量无偏好优化时的行为漂移。提出ADA协议和AAS诊断性选择器。

Result: 统计上在任务准确率方面不可区分的选择器在拒绝率、冗长度和谄媚行为上存在显著差异；高评分数据系统性地偏好更长、更坚定、更顺从或更倾向拒绝的行为，具体取决于在线评分定义；偏移方向可从所选数据的属性组合预测。

Conclusion: 在线数据选择是一种被忽视的隐式对齐机制，选择器的评分函数实际上扮演了奖励模型的角色。需要对齐感知的数据选择方法，AAS提供了在保持数据效率的同时约束安全与风格偏移的可行方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is often treated as a capability-adaptation step, while alignment is attributed to later preference optimization or reinforcement learning. This separation is incomplete: when examples are scored and kept online during fine-tuning, the choice of which data to train on already changes the model's behavioral preferences. We study online data selection as an implicit alignment mechanism. Given the same base model, optimizer, and selected-token budget, we compare random, loss-based, quality-based, and diversity-based online selectors and measure the behavioral drift they induce without any preference optimization. The proposed evaluation tracks helpfulness, refusal rate, verbosity, truthfulness, sycophancy, calibration, and jailbreak robustness, together with diagnostics for which behavioral modes are over-represented in the selected data. We formalize online selection as a reweighted SFT objective whose weights define an implicit preference over response styles and safety postures, so that an online scorer plays the role usually assigned to a reward model. This view predicts that high-scoring data can systematically favor longer, more assertive, more compliant, or more refusal-prone behaviors depending on how the online score is defined. Empirically, selectors that are statistically indistinguishable in task accuracy diverge sharply in refusal rate, verbosity, and sycophancy, and we show that the direction of the shift is predictable from the attribute mixture of the selected data. We introduce Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA), a controlled protocol for quantifying selection-induced behavioral movement, and Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS), a diagnostic online selector that retains data efficiency while constraining drift along safety and style axes.

</details>


### [53] [Latent graph encoding of multimodal neuroimaging features with generative AI architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07027)
*Ishaan Batta,Meenu Ajith,Vince Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性评估了多种生成框架（VAE、Transformer、GAN、扩散模型）用于多模态MRI特征（结构GMV和功能sFNC）的编码与融合，提出基于图编码的多模态图VAE（gMMVAE），在生成保真度、重建质量、效率和潜在空间可判别性上优于其他变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何设计最优的多模态生成框架，通过对编码策略、潜在多模态融合和生成模型选择的系统评估，实现对脑结构-功能MRI特征的有效编码、生成和重建。

Method: 利用大规模神经影像数据集的GMV与sFNC特征，系统比较VAE、Transformer、GAN和扩散模型等生成框架；提出模态感知图编码方法，将功能连接编码至低维潜在空间，并构建多模态图VAE（gMMVAE）。

Result: 采用模态感知图编码的架构优于向量化编码或直接数据空间方法；gMMVAE在生成保真度、重建质量、效率和潜在空间可判别性等多项指标上超越其他生成变体。

Conclusion: gMMVAE为鲁棒的多模态神经影像分析提供了有前景的生成框架，证明了图编码在功能连接处理中的优势以及多模态融合对提升模型性能的重要性。

Abstract: While generative models enable encoding of complex neuroimaging data for feature generation and reconstruction, developing optimal architectural frameworks with appropriate encoding and latent space processes is crucial for studying structural and functional properties of the brain. We design a multimodal generative framework for structural and functional magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) features through systematic evaluation of encoding strategies, latent multimodal fusion, and generative model selection. Using structural gray matter volume (GMV) and static functional network connectivity (sFNC) features from a large neuroimaging dataset, we analyze generative frameworks involving variational autoencoders (VAEs), transformers, generative adversarial networks (GANs), and diffusion models. Architectures that employ modality-aware graph encoding of functional connectivity into a lower-dimensional latent space outperform vectorized encoders or direct data space approaches. The proposed multimodal graph VAE (gMMVAE) surpasses alternative generative variants across multiple metrics for generation fidelity, reconstruction quality, efficiency, and latent space discriminability, highlighting its potential for robust multimodal neuroimaging analysis.

</details>


### [54] [Constrained Decoding for Diffusion Language Models via Efficient Inference over Finite Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07026)
*Meihua Dang,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向扩散语言模型的约束解码算法，通过将有限自动机视为图模型，实现了在任意有限自动机可表达约束下的精确均值场后验采样，支持贪婪/随机解码和并行块解码，并在Dream-7B和LLaDA-8B上多个任务中显著提升准确率，开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束解码系统专为自回归模型设计，依赖从左到右的生成方式，逐位置屏蔽无效token。扩散语言模型在每一步同时从全分解的均值场分布中采样多个位置，打破了这一假设，因此需要新的约束解码算法。

Method: 将有限自动机视为图模型，推导出约束均值场后验的精确且可处理的采样算法；利用算术电路理论的深度缩减技术，将采样深度从线性降低到对数级别；支持贪婪解码、随机解码、并行解码和任意重掩码调度。

Result: 在Dream-7B和LLaDA-8B上，函数调用（xLAM, BFCL）、规划（Sudoku, Countdown）、Text-to-SQL（Spider）、数学推理（GSM-Symbolic）等任务中取得显著准确率提升。例如BFCL-Live上Dream-7B贪婪解码从63.9%提升至71.5%，随机采样从22.3%提升至69.0%，开销低于5%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过构造保证约束满足，兼容多种解码范式，在扩散语言模型上实现了高效且精确的约束解码，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Constrained decoding is essential for serving LLMs, ensuring that generated outputs follow specific structures such as JSON schema-formatted function calls. Existing systems are designed for autoregressive models and assume left-to-right generation, masking out invalid next tokens at each step. Diffusion language models, however, break this assumption: they sample multiple positions simultaneously from a fully-factorized mean-field distribution at each denoising step. In this paper, we present an exact and tractable algorithm for sampling from the constrained mean-field posterior under any constraint expressible as a finite automaton. Viewing finite automata as graphical models, we obtain tractable representations of the constrained distribution that enable efficient inference. The approach guarantees constraint satisfaction by construction, supports both greedy and sampling-based decoding, and is compatible with parallel and block-wise decoding under arbitrary remasking schedules. Applying depth-reduction techniques from arithmetic circuit theory, we further reduce sampling depth from linear to logarithmic in the sequence length. Empirical evaluations on Dream-7B and LLaDA-8B show substantial accuracy gains across various tasks including function calling (xLAM, BFCL), planning (Sudoku, Countdown), text-to-SQL (Spider), and math reasoning (GSM-Symbolic), with little inference overhead relative to unconstrained decoding. For example, on BFCL-Live, our approach improves Dream-7B's greedy decoding accuracy from 63.9% to 71.5%, and stochastic sampling accuracy from 22.3% to 69.0%, where the unconstrained baseline collapses, with under 5% wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [55] [Gimitest: A Comprehensive Tool for Testing Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07029)
*Dennis Gross,Quentin Mazouni,Helge Spieker,Arnaud Gotlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gimitest，一个用于测试单/多智能体强化学习策略的综合性开源框架，支持多种Gym环境且可修改组件，在Farama Gymnasium和PettingZoo等环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化测试方法仅针对特定环境、测试场景和RL算法，导致RL策略的可靠性保障成为痛点，亟需一个通用的综合测试框架。

Method: 设计了一个支持单智能体和多智能体RL策略的综合测试框架，实现了开源工具Gimitest，支持多种gym框架并提供组件可修改性。

Result: 在官方Farama Gymnasium和PettingZoo等环境中成功测试了多种RL策略，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Gimitest为RL策略的可靠性测试提供了一个通用、灵活且有效的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies can be unsafe and vulnerable to attacks. Ensuring their reliability is often a pain point as existing automated testing methods target only selected environments, testing scenarios, and RL algorithms. To address this, we propose a comprehensive framework for testing single- and multi-agent RL policies under varying conditions. Our implementation of this framework, Gimitest, is an open-source tool that supports various gym frameworks and allows for modifications of their integrated components. This article describes the framework and details Gimitest's functionality and architecture. It showcases its effectiveness in testing multiple RL policies in environments such as the official Farama Gymnasium and PettingZoo.

</details>


### [56] [Intrinsic Green's Learning: Supervised Learning on Manifolds via Inverse PDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07034)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出内在格林学习（IGL）框架，将流形上的目标函数建模为线性PDE的解，通过可学习格林核积分实现高效低维积分分解，并自动发现流形的内在维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维流形上直接学习目标函数面临维度灾难，现有方法通常需要大量数据或陷入次优解。作者希望利用流形的低维内在结构，将复杂的高维积分高效分解为独立的一维积分。

Method: (1) 将目标函数视为线性PDE的解，通过格林核积分从学到的源函数重构目标；(2) 设计编码器发现流形的低维坐标，并在该坐标下将源和核分解为低秩张量，将高维积分转化为线性成本的独立一维积分；(3) 采用两阶段算法：先发现坐标，再进行近凸的线性源拟合，避免联合训练的维度坍塌；(4) 引入可学习门控自动发现内在维度。

Result: 在合成流形和MNIST数据集上验证，达到接近最优的分类性能，同时自动恢复数据的真实内在维度。

Conclusion: IGL提供了一种利用流形内在结构进行高效学习的新范式，将学习问题转化为线性PDE求解，兼具理论优雅性和实际有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Intrinsic Green's Learning (IGL), a framework that models a target function on a manifold as the solution to a linear PDE whose source term is learned from data. Rather than approximating the target directly, IGL learns a source and integrates it against a Green's kernel. An encoder discovers a low-dimensional coordinate chart on the manifold where both the source and the kernel decompose as low-rank tensors, collapsing a high-dimensional integral into independent one-dimensional integrals with cost linear in the intrinsic dimension. A two-stage algorithm separates coordinate discovery from source fitting, a near-convex linear solve, preventing the dimensional collapse of joint training. Learnable gates on each coordinate automatically discover the intrinsic dimension of the manifold. We validate IGL on synthetic manifolds and on MNIST, where it simultaneously achieves near-optimal classification and automatic recovery of the intrinsic dimension.

</details>


### [57] [On the Principles of Deep Feedforward ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07035)
*Changcun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多层深度前馈ReLU网络的工作机制，以路径概念为核心，揭示了其训练解的形成原理，从而在一定程度上打开了深度ReLU网络的黑箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度前馈神经网络在深度学习中无处不在，但其工作机制仍是一个黑箱。本文旨在从最简单的两层ReLU网络出发，系统性地理解多层深度ReLU网络的机制，揭示反向传播算法获得的训练解的内在原理。

Method: 以路径（path）及其关系为核心概念，从两层ReLU网络推广到多层情况。分析了深度ReLU网络中单元如何形成分段线性流形（而非超平面）来划分输入空间，并研究了如何高效利用隐藏层单元同时产生线性函数和输入空间划分。提出了深层情况下的多个严格偏序和连续性限制等原则。

Result: 证明了深度ReLU网络中的一个单元可以形成分段线性流形来划分输入空间（不同于两层的超平面情况）；表明两层ReLU网络的原理（如多个严格偏序、连续性限制）在很大程度上可以推广到深层情况；基本简单的原则组合可以产生包括训练解在内的复杂实例。

Conclusion: 通过路径关系等基本而简单的原理，成功解释了深度前馈ReLU网络的训练解，从而在一定程度上揭示了其黑箱机制。

Abstract: The architecture of deep feedforward neural networks is ubiquitous in deep learning, either as a whole system or as a subnetwork of other architectures, and thus its mechanism is a key ingredient of the black box of neural networks. On the basis of the simplest two-layer ReLU network, this paper systematically studies the mechanism of deep feedforward ReLU networks with multiple hidden layers and successfully explains the training solution obtained by the back-propagation algorithm. The concept of a path, especially in terms of the relationships between paths, plays a central role in uncovering the mystery of the black box. It is shown that a unit of a deep ReLU network can form a piecewise linear manifold to divide the input space, instead of a hyperplane of the two-layer case. How to efficiently use the hidden-layer units to produce both linear functions and partitions of the input space is also a central problem. The principles of a two-layer ReLU network can be generalized to the deeper case to a large extent, such as multiple strict partial orders and continuity restriction. The combination of the basic and simple principles proposed can yield complicated instantiations including the training solutions, and in this sense the black box of deep feedforward ReLU networks is revealed.

</details>


### [58] [Gauge-Invariant Learnable Spectral Positional Encodings for Directed Graphs via Hermitian Block Krylov Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07032)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于磁Krylov子空间的可学习谱位置编码方法，通过矩阵函数避免规范依赖，仅需稀疏矩阵-向量乘积即可在$O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$步内收敛，在有向图任务中显著优于方向盲PE，在无向异质图上也超越无PE和多项式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图的谱位置编码面临两个障碍：磁拉普拉斯算子需要$O(n^3)$的厄米特征分解，且复特征向量存在酉规范不确定性，之前的工作需用基不变架构处理。

Method: 提出可学习谱PE形式$h_θ(A_q)R$，其中$A_q$为归一化磁算子，$h_θ$为可学习标量谱响应，$R$为随机探测块。在厄米块Krylov子空间中仅通过稀疏矩阵-向量乘积计算，证明了$k=O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$步块步骤足以在热-预解响应族上一致收敛。

Result: 在有向SBM上，方向盲PE表现如随机，而磁Krylov PE随深度增加收敛到精确特征分解预言机；相同探测以$1/\sqrt{s}$蒙特卡洛误差产生规范不变成对特征；无向$q=0$情形在异质图基准上优于无PE和多项式基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过矩阵函数构造天然规范不变性，避免显式特征分解，具有理论保证的收敛速度，并在有向图和无向异质图上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Spectral positional encodings (PEs) for \emph{directed} graphs face two obstacles: magnetic Laplacians require an $O(n^3)$ Hermitian eigendecomposition per potential, and their complex eigenvectors are defined only up to unitary gauge, which prior work handles with basis-invariant architectures. We propose learnable spectral PEs of the form $h_θ(A_q)\,R$, where $A_q$ is a normalized magnetic operator, $h_θ$ a learnable scalar spectral response, and $R$ a block of random probes. Because the PE is a \emph{matrix function} of the operator, it is gauge-invariant by construction. We compute it in a Hermitian block Krylov subspace from sparse matrix--vector products only, prove that $k = O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ block steps suffice uniformly over heat--resolvent response families, and give a covering-number argument for why low-dimensional structured families generalize where free per-eigenvalue weights overfit. On a directed SBM whose symmetrization is uninformative by construction, direction-blind PEs stay at chance while magnetic Krylov PEs converge to the exact-eigendecomposition oracle as the depth grows. The same probes yield gauge-invariant pairwise features with $1/\sqrt{s}$ Monte-Carlo error, and the undirected $q{=}0$ case improves heterophilous benchmarks over no-PE and polynomial baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Complexity-Budgeted, Interaction-Aware Interpretable Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07060)
*Srikumar Krishnamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IAIML是一个面向表格数据的可解释机器学习框架，通过自适应离散化、有限网格成对交互评分和分区解释预算三个机制，在保持模型可解释性的同时捕获特征交互信息，在40个数据集上达到接近梯度提升集成模型的性能（平均AUC差距1.4点），而解释组件数量仅为后者的1/14至1/28。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可解释分类器在特征筛选阶段会丢弃那些单独预测能力弱但通过与其他变量联合配置才具有预测价值的变量，导致信息损失。现有方法缺乏对特征交互的显式处理机制。

Method: IAIML框架包含三个协调机制：(1) 自适应逐特征离散化；(2) 有限网格成对交互评分；(3) 分区解释预算。检测到的交互通过两种策略处理：放松筛选阈值让交互支持的变量进入模式搜索，或为稀疏下游分类器构建显式配对项。

Result: 在40个数据集（24个真实基准+16个合成交互压力测试）上，经嵌套交叉验证评估，IAIML达到平均AUC与调优后的梯度提升集成模型仅差1.4点，解释组件数量减少14-28倍。在强成对交互结构、低边际信号的数据集上超越所有基线。与RuleFit在AUC和组件数量上相当且调参成本更低。EBM在完整面板上有小幅显著AUC优势但查找表开销更大。在需要高阶交互的数据集上性能下降。

Conclusion: IAIML是一个紧凑的、交互感知的可解释框架，适用于对解释规模有约束且需要受控处理特征交互的场景。消融实验证实自适应离散化和交互感知准入机制均有增量贡献。

Abstract: Inherently interpretable classifiers for tabular data typically rely on sparse features, rules, or patterns that users can inspect directly. The marginal feature-screening step common to these methods can discard variables whose predictive value emerges only through joint configurations with other variables. We present Interaction Aware Interpretable Machine Learning (IAIML), a framework that addresses this limitation through three coordinated mechanisms: adaptive per-feature discretization, finite-grid pairwise interaction scoring, and a partitioned explanation budget. Detected interactions are routed through one of two strategies: relaxing the screening filter so that interaction-supported variables enter the pattern search, or constructing explicit pair terms for a sparse downstream classifier. On a 40-dataset panel comprising 24 real-world tabular benchmarks and 16 synthetic interaction stress tests, evaluated under nested cross-validation, IAIML achieves mean AUC within 1.4 points of tuned gradient-boosted ensembles while requiring roughly 14--28 times fewer fitted explanation components. On datasets with strong pairwise interaction structure and low marginal signal, IAIML outperforms all baselines. Among compact interpretable methods, IAIML is comparable to RuleFit in AUC and component count and is less expensive to tune. EBM obtains a small but significant AUC advantage across the full panel, with a substantially larger lookup-table footprint. Performance degrades on datasets requiring higher-order interactions beyond the pairwise scope. Component-isolated ablations confirm that adaptive discretization and interaction-aware admission each contribute incrementally. These results support IAIML as a compact, interaction-aware framework appropriate for settings where bounded explanation size and controlled treatment of feature interactions are design requirements.

</details>


### [60] [Multiplication Beyond Groups: Stratified Fourier Mechanisms in Transformer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07066)
*Zitong Andrew Chen,Junaid Hasan,Akhil Srinivasan,Hemkesh Bandi,Jarod Alper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小型Transformer如何学习模合数乘法（含零因子的非可逆运算），提出"幺半群扩展"机制：模型将输入空间划分为局部层级代数区域，在每个区域内应用类群结构和傅里叶机制，从而将表示论方法从群运算推广到更一般的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注Transformer在全局可逆运算（如循环加法、群组合）上的算法推理机制，但对非可逆运算（如模合数乘法，存在零因子）的理解尚不充分。作者希望探究Transformer是否以及如何将群表示机制推广到更一般的代数结构。

Method: 提出"幺半群扩展"（monoid extension）框架，作为群组合通过表示（GCR）的局部化推广。在平方自由模合数乘法上训练小型Transformer，通过分析嵌入组织方式、注意力路由模式、低秩写入方向以及局部特征对输出logits的贡献来验证该框架。

Result: 嵌入围绕局部层级代数区域组织；注意力展现出类别敏感的路由和低秩写入方向；局部特征解释模型输出logits的很大一部分。

Conclusion: 表示论机制可以从群运算扩展到更一般的代数结构（如幺半群），模型不依赖单一全局表示空间，而是通过局部层级划分来适应非可逆运算。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated a remarkable ability to learn algorithmic reasoning, yet mechanistic analyses have mostly focused on globally invertible operations such as cyclic addition and group composition. In this work, we investigate how small transformers learn modular integer multiplication over composite moduli, a fundamentally non-invertible operation due to the presence of zero-divisors. We propose the monoid extension: a localized generalization of Group Composition via Representation (GCR) that suggests the learned computation does not rely on a single global representation space. Instead, the model partitions the input space into local hierarchical algebraic regions, where group-like structure survives and Fourier mechanisms can be applied. In transformers trained on square-free modular multiplication, we find that embeddings organize around these regions, attention exhibits class-sensitive routing and low-rank write directions, and local character features explain a large fraction of the model's output logits. Our results suggest that representation-theoretic mechanisms previously identified for group operations can extend beyond groups to more general structures.

</details>


### [61] [Entropy Pacing Policy Optimization for Multi-Task Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07178)
*Zetian Hu,Shunyu Liu,Junjie Zhang,Yongcheng Jing,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多任务强化学习智能体场景中不同任务间“探索-利用”节奏不匹配的问题，提出熵节奏策略优化（EPPO），通过任务级动态裁剪机制替代GRPO的固定裁剪阈值，在多任务智能体基准测试上取得优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习用于大语言模型智能体的工作大多集中在单任务场景，而实际部署需要多任务通用智能体。在多任务智能体RL中，不同任务存在探索-利用节奏不匹配：简单任务过早收敛到低熵策略，阻碍困难任务学习；困难任务又可能将简单任务推回高熵探索状态，产生熵交叉和熵尖峰，这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出熵节奏策略优化（EPPO），核心是任务级动态裁剪机制：用任务熵感知的自适应边界替代GRPO中的固定裁剪阈值，对过度自信（低熵）的任务收紧更新，对探索不足（高熵）的任务放松更新，从而协调多任务间的熵分布，稳定优化过程。

Result: 在多任务智能体基准测试上，EPPO取得了优于对比方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过协调任务间熵的节奏，EPPO有效缓解了多任务RL中的探索-利用节奏不匹配问题，为训练多任务通用智能体LLM提供了更稳定的优化方案。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs of Reinforcement Learning (RL) have highlighted its potential for complex agentic Large Language Model (LLM) tasks. However, existing efforts largely focus on single-task settings, whereas real-world deployment necessitates a generalist agent capable of solving multiple tasks simultaneously. In this work, we identify a critical yet underexplored phenomenon in multi-task agentic RL: different tasks can exhibit exploration-exploitation pace mismatch. Specifically, easier tasks may converge early to low-entropy policies that hinder learning on harder tasks, while harder tasks can, in turn, push easier tasks back toward high-entropy exploration. This back-and-forth interaction creates inter-task entropy crossovers and frequent entropy spikes. Inspired by this observation, we introduce Entropy Pacing Policy Optimization (EPPO) for multi-task agentic LLMs, which coordinates entropy across tasks to stabilize multi-task optimization. At the core of EPPO is a task-wise dynamic clipping mechanism that replaces the fixed clipping threshold in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a task entropy-aware adaptive bound, tightening updates for over-confident tasks while relaxing them for under-explored ones. Experiments on the multi-task agentic benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed EPPO yields results superior to its counterparts.

</details>


### [62] [Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07184)
*Marcus Williams,Hannah Sheahan,Cameron Raymond,Tomek Korbak,Deng Pan,Peilin Yang,Leon Maksin,Ningyi Xie,Phillip Guo,Ian Kivlichan,Micah Carroll*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"部署模拟"(deployment simulation)的方法，通过从先前模型部署中获取的去标识化对话，固定对话前缀并用候选模型重新生成响应，从而在部署前模拟模型的真实行为表现。实验基于GPT-5系列模型，结果表明该方法能有效估计部署后的错误行为率，优于传统评估方法，且可通过公开聊天数据种子化，为外部研究者提供了无需私有日志即可进行部署级评估的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预部署安全评估存在覆盖率不足、缺乏代表性、容易被模型识别为测试等问题，难以准确预测模型在真实部署环境中的行为风险。作者希望设计一种更贴近真实部署场景的评估方法，以更可靠地估计模型在实际使用中的错误行为发生率。

Method: 从先前模型部署的去标识化对话中提取初始对话前缀，保持前缀不变，用候选模型重新生成后续响应。生成的响应一方面可用于审计新型对齐问题，另一方面可用于估计部署前模型错误行为的普遍性。实验在GPT-5系列部署上进行了评估，使用注册的结果盲预测（针对GPT-5.4），并对三个早期版本进行回顾性分析。

Result: 部署模拟方法能生成有信息量的后部署错误行为率估计，且优于基于对抗性选择的基线生产数据方法；其对评估感知的估计比传统评估更接近生产流量数据；工具重采样(tool resampling)的真实性是提高预测的主要挑战；该方法可从公开聊天数据中种子化，仍能提供有信息量的生产错误行为率估计。

Conclusion: 部署模拟能够帮助评估者预测语言模型在真实世界中的行为，支持更定量的部署风险评估，并为外部研究者提供了无需私有生产日志即可进行部署级评估的可行路径。

Abstract: Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.

</details>


### [63] [ORCAID: Oblique Rule-Based Continuous-Action Interpretation for Deep RL Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07235)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Ezio Bartocci,Thomas Eiter,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCAID是一种从连续动作空间的强化学习智能体中提取可解释规则策略的方法，通过高效的斜决策树训练算法（随机初始化、局部优化、后向消除与叶合并）来学习状态空间的分割与局部线性模型，在保持性能的同时显著降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的可解释性仍然是一个关键问题，尤其在连续动作空间中，从复杂环境中训练的智能体提取可解释策略极具挑战性。

Method: 提出ORCAID方法，核心是一个高效的斜决策树训练算法，包含三个阶段的分割搜索：随机初始化、局部优化和后向消除。最后通过合并相邻叶节点生成一组简洁的可解释规则。

Result: 在多个强化学习环境中，提取的基于规则的策略以较少的参数保持了强性能，甚至可用于提升原始深度强化学习策略的表现。

Conclusion: ORCAID能够有效从连续动作空间的RL智能体中提取可解释规则策略，同时保持甚至提升原始策略的性能。

Abstract: Explainability remains a key issue in reinforcement learning (RL). Distilling an interpretable policy from an agent trained in a complex environment is particularly challenging when the action space is continuous. We introduce ORCAID, a novel method for extracting interpretable rule-based policies from RL agents operating in mixed continuous-discrete environments with continuous action spaces. Our main contribution is an efficient oblique decision tree training algorithm that partitions the state space by hyperplanes and fits local linear models. The key idea lies in a three-stage split search: efficient random initialization, local refinement, and backward elimination. Finally, adjacent leaves are merged to yield a concise set of interpretable rules describing a given deep RL policy. We evaluate ORCAID across multiple RL environments, demonstrating that the extracted rule-based policies maintain strong performance with a low number of parameters and can even be used to improve the performance of the original deep RL policy.

</details>


### [64] [An Hybrid Quantum-Classical Diffusion Model for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07072)
*Qipeng Qian,Keli Deng,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合生成管线，结合经典自编码器进行降维与混合态量子去噪扩散概率模型(MSQuDDPM)在潜在空间中学习，通过预测干净状态ρ₀并利用解析反向传播规则简化逆向动力学，在有限量子比特预算下实现混合量子-经典生成建模，并在MNIST上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子扩散模型在量子态上直接进行噪声与去噪，具备物理一致性，但应用于经典高维数据时受限于状态编码的量子比特成本和模拟大型密度算子的计算负担。

Method: 1) 使用经典自编码器将高维数据压缩为低维潜在编码，嵌入到小量子比特希尔伯特空间；2) 在潜在空间中运行混合态量子去噪扩散概率模型(MSQuDDPM)；3) 通过预测时间步t的干净状态ρ₀，并利用解析反向传播规则计算单步逆向更新，替代学习显式的ρ_{t-1}预测器。

Result: 在MNIST图像生成任务上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合态量子扩散可作为混合量子-经典生成建模的实用骨干，在现实量子比特预算下具备可行性。

Abstract: Quantum diffusion models provide a physics-consistent route to generative learning by formulating noising and denoising directly on quantum states. However, applying such models to classical high-dimensional data is constrained by the qubit cost of state encoding and the computational burden of simulating large density operators. We propose a scalable hybrid generative pipeline that combines a classical autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a mixed-state quantum denoising diffusion probabilistic model (MSQuDDPM) operating in the learned latent space. The autoencoder compresses data into compact latent codes that can be embedded into a small-qubit Hilbert space, after which the quantum diffusion model learns a generative distribution over latent density operators and decodes samples back to the original domain. Algorithmically, we simplify the reverse dynamics by predicting an estimate of the clean state $ρ_0$ at timestep $t$ and computing the one-step reverse update via an analytic backward propagation rule, rather than learning an explicit predictor for $ρ_{t-1}$. We demonstrate the proposed approach on MNIST image generation and discuss how mixed-state quantum diffusion can serve as a practical backbone for hybrid quantum--classical generative modeling under realistic qubit budgets.

</details>


### [65] [FMMVCC: Fuzzy Mamba-based Multi-View Contrastive Clustering for Univariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07258)
*Donato Cerciello,Leonardo Schiavo,Angel Panizo-LLedot,Javier Huertas Tato,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Mamba的深度时间序列聚类框架FMMVCC，通过状态空间序列建模实现线性复杂度的长程时序依赖捕捉，结合多视图自监督学习，在15个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量时间序列数据标注昂贵或有限，现有深度聚类方法难以捕捉长程时序依赖或计算成本过高，需要高效的无监督聚类方法。

Method: 提出FMMVCC框架：采用Mamba（基于状态空间序列模型）高效学习时序表示，具有线性计算复杂度；结合多视图自监督学习（时间掩码与数据增强）。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上的实验评估中，FMMVCC在60个总指标评估中取得29个最佳表现，在所有测试场景中平均排名最高。

Conclusion: FMMVCC作为一种高效的Mamba-based深度聚类框架，在时间序列聚类任务上显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Abstract: In many realistic scenarios, large volumes of time series data are generated with limited or expensive annotations. This limitation makes supervised learning methods difficult to apply and leads to the use of unsupervised approaches capable of discovering meaningful structures directly from raw data. Clustering therefore plays a crucial role in organizing time series into groups that share similar temporal patterns, enabling exploratory analysis and downstream tasks without requiring manual labeling. However, existing deep clustering methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies or rely on architectures with high computational cost. This paper introduces FMMVCC, a Mamba-based deep clustering framework for time series that leverages state space sequence modeling to efficiently learn temporal representations with linear complexity. Additionally, it utilizes multi-view self-supervised learning with temporal masking and augmentations. Experimental evaluation in 15 benchmark datasets proves that FMMVCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the best overall performance in 29 of 60 total metric evaluations and the highest average rank in all tested scenarios.

</details>


### [66] [Structural Adversarial Attacks on Relational Deep Learning under Integrity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07089)
*Alan Gany,Bogdan Cautis,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了关系深度学习（RDL）流程的对抗鲁棒性，针对白盒攻击者通过重写外键引用（保持数据库完整性约束）进行图扰动，提出了七种攻击启发式方法，实验表明梯度攻击在回归任务上优于随机基线，但在分类任务上增益较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系深度学习已成为关系数据库上机器学习的主流方法，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。理解攻击者如何利用外键约束进行有限但有效的扰动，对于评估和改进RDL管线的安全性至关重要。

Method: 考虑白盒攻击者，知道图构建和模型训练方式，但只能通过重写外键引用来扰动上游数据库，且必须保持外键有效性、度为一的外键约束和函数依赖等完整性约束。在全局扰动预算下，提出了七种攻击启发式方法：两个随机采样基线和五个利用可微边缘掩码的梯度引导变体，并在RelBench rel-f1基准上进行评估。

Result: 基于梯度的攻击在回归任务上持续优于随机基线，但在分类任务上增益较小，原因是分类任务标签翻转率低且分类输出具有更大的局部稳定性。

Conclusion: 关系深度学习的对抗鲁棒性存在漏洞，尤其在回归任务中更易受到梯度引导的攻击；分类任务相对更鲁棒。研究揭示了在保持数据库完整性约束的前提下进行对抗攻击的可能性与局限性。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) has become a standard methodology for machine learning on relational databases: the database is encoded as a heterogeneous temporal graph in which tuples become nodes and primary-key to foreign-key (PK-FK) dependencies become typed edges, over which a graph neural network is trained for downstream prediction. We study the adversarial robustness of this pipeline. We consider a white-box attacker who knows how the graph is built and the model is trained, reasons about perturbations on the graph, but can only act on the upstream database, by rewiring foreign-key references while preserving the integrity constraints of the schema (foreign-key validity, the degree-one FK constraint, and functional dependencies). This restricts the attacker to a constrained, combinatorial set of admissible edits under a global perturbation budget, which is intractable to explore exhaustively and made non-additive by GNN message passing. We investigate seven attack heuristics - two random sampling baselines and five gradient-guided variants that exploit differentiable edge masks - and evaluate them on the RelBench rel-f1 benchmark. Gradient-based attacks consistently outperform random baselines on regression tasks, whereas gains on classification are smaller, which we attribute to low label-flip rates and greater local stability of classification outputs.

</details>


### [67] [FedCVESA: Taking Away Training Data in Federated Learning via Correlation Value Encoding and Segmented Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07314)
*Chongkai Li,Bang Zhang,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCVESA攻击方法，证明联邦学习在恶意服务器场景下可被用作参数级记忆通道，通过相关性正则化将训练数据编码进全局模型参数，并利用分段聚合防止参数被覆盖，最终在非独立同分布数据上成功窃取语义级别的训练图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式TATD攻击表明恶意训练可利用深度模型记忆能力存储并恢复训练数据，但该记忆化威胁在联邦学习环境中尚未被系统研究，因为多客户端平均可能覆盖编码的训练数据。

Method: 提出FedCVESA攻击方法：1) 恶意服务器从K个参与客户中选择n个目标客户端，在目标客户端损失函数中添加皮尔逊相关性正则化项，将私有训练数据逐步编码到选定的载体参数中；2) 提出分段聚合策略，在服务器聚合时保留选定的载体参数，对其他参数保持标准平均，以减少载体参数被覆盖。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，使用狄利克雷非独立同分布划分的实验表明，该方法能从训练后的模型中窃取具有语义意义的私有训练图像，同时保持可接受的主任务效用。

Conclusion: 联邦学习可以成为主动TATD攻击的参数级记忆通道，在白盒恶意服务器设置下存在严重隐私泄露风险。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) avoids explicit data exposure by keeping raw data on local clients, yet privacy risks remain in the training process and the learned model itself. Recently, centralized Taking Away Training Data (TATD) attacks have shown that malicious training could abuse the memorization capacity of deep models to store and later recover training data. However, this memorization-based threat has not been systematically studied under FL environments, where multi-client averaging could overwrite encoded training data. In this paper, we study a white-box TATD attack in which a malicious server selects n target clients from K participating clients and actively writes private training data into the global model during federated training. We propose FedCVESA, a federated variant of Correlation Value Encoding Attack (CVEA), by adding a Pearson-correlation regularizer to the loss function of target clients, so that private training data are gradually encoded into selected model parameters, referred to as carrier parameters. To reduce the overwriting of carrier parameters during server aggregation, we further propose segmented aggregation over dispersed carrier parameters, preserving selected carrier parameters while keeping standard averaging on the remaining parameters. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 under Dirichlet non-IID partitions show that the proposed method can steal semantically meaningful private training images from the trained model while maintaining acceptable main-task utility in a controlled proof-of-concept setting. These results demonstrate that FL can become a parameter-level memorization channel for active TATD attack under the studied white-box malicious-server setting.

</details>


### [68] [A knowledge-augmented dataset of high-risk driving scenarios with LLM annotations for autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07103)
*Heye Huang,Jingguang Li,Zhiyuan Zhou,Paul Liang,Mingyu Wu,Kitae Jang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-Risk是一个知识增强数据集，整合了20个人类驾驶和自动驾驶轨迹数据集（涵盖欧洲、中国、美国），通过统一的风险提取流程筛选出31,398个高风险事件（含1,036个近碰撞极端事件），每个事件以同步轨迹、元数据和多维度语言三元组（场景描述、异常行为通知、因果风险分析及可验证行动建议）形式提供，旨在弥合结构化轨迹、语义推理与决策监督之间的鸿沟，为风险感知自动驾驶智能体提供标准化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然驾驶数据中高风险长尾场景严重不足，已有的轨迹和语言增强数据集缺乏高风险事件标签、语义注释和可验证的安全信号，亟需一个结合结构化轨迹与语义推理的高质量风险场景数据集。

Method: 采用统一的风险中心提取流程，整合20个来自欧中美的人类驾驶和自动驾驶轨迹数据集；利用大语言模型生成语义注释；每个事件以轨迹、元数据和语言三元组（场景描述、异常行为通知、因果分析及行动建议）形式发布；通过闭环模拟器迭代反思验证代表性子集。

Result: 构建了包含31,398个高风险事件（其中1,036个近碰撞极端事件）的数据集，每个事件具备多维度风险标注、可解释语言监督和可验证决策信息。

Conclusion: K-Risk通过结合结构化交通轨迹、语义推理和决策监督，为开发和评估下一代风险感知自动驾驶智能体提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Safe autonomous driving requires both rapid responses to common high-risk events and deeper reasoning over rare, extreme long-tail scenarios in traffic safety. These scenarios are severely under-represented in naturalistic driving data, and existing trajectory and language-augmented datasets seldom provide high-risk event labels, semantic annotations, and verifiable safety signals. Here we present K-Risk, a knowledge-augmented dataset that combines structured driving trajectories with large language model generated semantic annotations for safety-critical driving scenarios. K-Risk integrates 20 human-driven and autonomous-vehicle trajectory datasets from Europe, China, and the United States, covering highways, urban freeways, intersections, and roundabouts. Using a unified risk-centric extraction pipeline, K-Risk curates 31,398 high-risk events, together with a 1,036-event extreme subset of near-collision cases. Each event is released as a synchronized trajectory, metadata, and language triplet containing structured scenario descriptions, abnormal-behavior notifications, and, for a representative subset, causal risk analyses and action recommendations validated through a closed-loop simulator with iterative reflection. By combining multi-dimensional risk annotations, interpretable language supervision, and verifiable decisions, K-Risk bridges structured traffic trajectories, semantic reasoning, and decision supervision, providing a standardized foundation for developing and evaluating next-generation risk-aware autonomous driving agents.

</details>


### [69] [Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion: An SDE Framework for Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07330)
*Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Dengyi Zhao,Xingqin Qi,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperNSD，一种基于随机微分方程的超图不确定性估计框架，通过将超图表示建模为随机过程，利用可学习漂移和随机强迫函数捕获高阶扩散动力学与结构不确定性，实现预测不确定性的内在量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络在处理高阶关系时表现出强大能力，但其预测不确定性尚未充分探索。超图中的不确定性不仅来自噪声属性和模糊标签，还来自节点-超边关联结构的变异和复杂的高阶依赖。现有方法主要从最终预测估计不确定性或依赖计算昂贵的集成和贝叶斯推断，难以在表示学习过程中捕获不确定性的演化。

Method: 提出Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion (HyperNSD)，核心创新包括：1) 将超图表示建模为在节点-超边关联结构上演化的随机过程；2) 可学习漂移函数捕获确定性高阶扩散动力学；3) 可学习随机强迫函数刻画结构模糊性和表示噪声；4) 通过随机表示轨迹的变异性直接量化预测不确定性；5) 神经漂移和扩散网络实现预测与不确定性传播的联合学习。

Result: 在多个超图基准上，HyperNSD在分布外检测和误分类检测中实现了可靠的不确定性估计，同时保持了竞争性的预测精度。理论分析建立了随机动力学的适定性、扰动稳定性、置换等变性和数值收敛性。

Conclusion: HyperNSD为可信赖的高阶表示学习提供了一个原则性的随机动力学框架，有效填补了超图神经网络不确定性估计的研究空白。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks have shown powerful capability in modeling higher-order relations, yet their predictive uncertainty remains underexplored. Unlike pairwise graphs, uncertainty in hypergraphs arises not only from noisy attributes and ambiguous labels, but also from variations in node-hyperedge incidence structures and complex higher-order dependencies. Existing approaches mainly estimate uncertainty from final predictions or rely on computationally expensive ensembles and Bayesian inference, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty evolution during representation learning. In this paper, we propose Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion(HyperNSD), a stochastic differential equation framework for uncertainty estimation on hypergraphs. HyperNSD models hypergraph representations as stochastic processes evolving over node-hyperedge incidence structures. A learnable drift function captures deterministic higher-order diffusion dynamics, while a learnable stochastic forcing function characterizes structural ambiguity and representation noise. Predictive uncertainty is directly quantified through the variability of stochastic representation trajectories, providing an intrinsic uncertainty measure beyond post-hoc confidence scores. We formulate HyperNSD with neural drift and diffusion networks, enabling joint learning of prediction and uncertainty propagation. Theoretical analyses establish well posedness, perturbation stability,permutation equivariance, and numerical convergence of the proposed stochastic dynamics. Experiments on multiple hypergraph benchmarks demonstrate that HyperNSD achieves reliable uncertainty estimation for out-of-distribution and misclassification detection while preserving competitive prediction accuracy. These results provide a principled stochastic-dynamical framework for trustworthy higher-order representation learning.

</details>


### [70] [Distributed Sparse Interventions in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07128)
*Maximilian S. Ernst,Lorenz Linhardt,Aaron Peikert,Oliver Eberle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式稀疏干预（DSI）方法，通过在神经元层面进行稀疏、分布式的因果干预，仅干预0.01%的神经元即可有效激活指令微调语言模型中的任务行为，揭示了神经元层面非线性和交互效应的重要性，并实现了对模型行为的精细控制与任务相关神经元的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型引导方法主要关注激活空间中全局方向上的线性加性干预，忽略了神经元层面的非线性效应和跨层交互，无法充分捕捉任务表示的复杂性。

Method: 提出分布式稀疏干预（DSI），在神经元层面考虑非线性和跨层神经元交互，识别稀疏的神经元集合进行干预，实现任务相关计算的有效激活。

Result: 在多种任务上，DSI仅需干预0.01%的神经元即可激活指令微调语言模型的任务行为，表明稀疏分布式干预在神经元基上的有效性。

Conclusion: DSI通过稀疏干预实现了对模型行为的精细控制、任务相关神经元集合的定位，并加深了对任务组合的理解。

Abstract: Language models perform a wide range of tasks at varying levels of abstraction with the capacity to flexibly infer tasks from context, execute multiple tasks simultaneously, and select among competing tasks. To study the role of model components in task behaviour, their causal influence can be investigated through interventions. Prior work on model steering has largely focused on interventions along global directions in activation space, modeling task representations as approximately linear and additive. By studying interventions at the neuron level, we find substantial, neuron-specific nonlinear effects on model outputs that are not captured by current steering approaches. We introduce Distributed Sparse Interventions (DSI), an intervention approach that considers nonlinearities and interactions between neurons across layers to identify sparse sets of neurons that elicit task-relevant computations. Across a range of tasks, we demonstrate that DSI can activate task behaviour in instruction-tuned language models by localising and intervening on as few as 0.01% of neurons, highlighting the effectiveness of sparse, distributed interventions in the neuron basis. Additionally, adopting a set-based perspective enables computations over the identified neuron sets, offering insights into the roles of individual neurons by analysing their effects across tasks. Through sparse interventions, DSI enables fine-grained control over model behaviour, localisation of task-relevant neuron sets, and furthers our understanding of task composition.

</details>


### [71] [Latency-Aware Bid Acceptance under Operational Feasibility: A Public Benchmark with Hindsight Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07343)
*Aswin Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FreightBidBench，一个公开可校准、无依赖的闭环基准测试，用于在线卡车货运报价接受问题。该基准基于公共数据，明确了可行性约束和经济参数，并开发了两种全视野事后上限（LP松弛和拉格朗日信息松弛），后者比LP松弛紧密度提升20.7%-39.3%。同时提出了一种参数化代理-rollout级联策略，在保持rollout利润94.2%-98%的同时，将决策延迟降低至40-56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线卡车货运报价接受问题缺乏公开、可复现的基准测试：现有路由基准是静态的，而动态车队研究通常依赖于私有运营数据，阻碍了算法比较与学术进展。

Method: 基于公共数据（Freight Analysis Framework和美国农业部卡车费率数据）构建闭环基准；开发两种全视野事后上限：LP松弛和拉格朗日-每卡车信息松弛；提出参数化代理-rollout级联策略，包含边界带和稀缺压力 escalation 触发机制。

Result: 拉格朗日信息松弛在紧容量场景下比LP松弛紧20.7%，在稀缺容量场景下紧39.3%；最佳简单策略保留rollout利润的91.0%和86.5%；级联策略在40-56%的决策延迟下恢复约98%的利润，在紧容量场景下与rollout教师策略在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: FreightBidBench为在线卡车货运报价接受问题提供了首个公开、可复现的基准；所提出的级联策略在接近最优利润的同时显著降低计算延迟，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Online truckload bid acceptance is a closed-loop stochastic decision problem in which a carrier or broker must, in real time, accept or reject a tendered load subject to operational feasibility, fleet repositioning costs, and opportunity cost against future demand. Public, reproducible benchmarks for this problem are scarce: existing routing benchmarks are static, while dynamic-fleet studies typically rely on private operator data. We introduce FreightBidBench, a public-calibrated, dependency-free, closed-loop benchmark in which feasibility (pickup reach, appointment windows, simplified hours-of-service, stochastic yard delays) and economics (service-failure penalty, terminal fleet value, daily price-premium window) are explicit, versioned, and reproducible from public Freight Analysis Framework and U.S. Department of Agriculture truck rate data. We develop two full-horizon hindsight ceilings: a simple LP style relaxation and a tighter Lagrangian-per-truck information relaxation that retains per-truck hours-of-service and sequencing structure and is 20.7% tighter than the LP relaxation on a tight-capacity scenario and 39.3% tighter on a scarce-capacity scenario. We introduce a parametric surrogate-rollout cascade with boundary-band and scarcity-pressure escalation triggers. On ten-seed tight and scarce scenarios, the best simple policy retains 91.0% and 86.5% of rollout profit and the standard-library surrogate 94.2% and 89.3%; a cascade at a single escalation band recovers roughly 98% on both at 40-56% of rollout's mean decision latency, and on the tight scenario is statistically indistinguishable from the rollout teacher (paired-bootstrap 95% CI on the profit delta spans zero).

</details>


### [72] [Fractal KV-Cache Archives: Lossless Symbolic Storage with In-Place Retrieval for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07144)
*Vladimir Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究KV缓存压缩后的存储问题，提出使用收缩迭代映射编码（类似混沌系统）将量化后的KV缓存符号流序列化为低维实向量，实现无损、线性时间、O(1)随机访问和O(1)均摊追加的存储格式，并证明该存档同时可作为子串搜索索引。在GPT-2 1024 token上下文实验中，结合精确小窗口（4个注意力锚点+32个最近token）和残差向量量化，存档KV缓存比fp16压缩36-54倍，困惑度损失11-15%，并发现量化键比量化值损害大约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是长上下文自回归推理的主要内存瓶颈，现有工作集中在量化、驱逐或卸载来压缩缓存，但忽视了压缩后符号流的存储问题，以及存储层能否承担更多功能（如搜索）。

Method: 采用收缩迭代映射编码（contractive iterated-map codes）将量化后的符号序列序列化为低维实向量，形成存档格式；结合精确小窗口（4个注意力锚点+32个最近token）和每头残差向量量化；利用键/值不对称性进行混合比特分配；存档向量直接支持近似子串查询。

Result: 存档KV缓存比fp16压缩36-54倍，困惑度损失11-15%；量化键的损害约为量化值的4倍；存档格式支持O(1)随机访问和O(1)均摊追加；近似子串查询可直接在存储向量上执行。

Conclusion: 收缩迭代映射编码是一种自然的KV缓存存档格式，兼具高效存储和搜索功能，键/值不对称性可用于优化比特分配。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates the memory cost of long-context autoregressive inference, and a growing body of work compresses it through quantization, eviction, or offloading. We study a complementary question: once a position's KV state has been quantized to codebook indices, how should the resulting symbol stream be stored, and can the storage layer do more than store? A family of contractive iterated-map codes that serialize a symbol sequence into a sequence of low-dimensional real vectors is revisited, and it is shown that they form a natural archive format for a quantized KV cache with the following features. The method provides exactly the access pattern a growing cache requires. It is lossless, it runs in linear time, and supports O(1) random access and O(1) amortized append. A controlled study of the quantizer feeding this archive is conducted on GPT-2 with 1024-token contexts.
